QueueStatus) => {\n  switch (status) {\n    case \"Queued\":\n    case \"Uploading\":\n    case \"Pending\":\n      
return \"bg-blue-100 text-blue-700 border-blue-200\";\n    case \"Processing\":\n    case \"AI Analysis\":\n    case 
\"OCR Running\":\n      return \"bg-amber-100 text-amber-700 border-amber-200\";\n    case \"Completed\":\n      
return \"bg-green-100 text-green-700 border-green-200\";\n    case \"Failed\":\n      return \"bg-red-100 text-red-700 
border-red-200\";\n    case \"Cancelled\":\n      return \"bg-gray-100 text-gray-700 border-gray-200\";\n    
default:\n      return \"bg-gray-100 text-gray-700 border-gray-200\";\n  }\n};\n\nconst getPriorityColor = (priority: 
Priority) => {\n  switch (priority) {\n    case \"High\": return \"bg-red-50 text-red-700\";\n    case \"Medium\": 
return \"bg-orange-50 text-orange-700\";\n    case \"Low\": return \"bg-green-50 text-green-700\";\n  }\n};\n\nconst 
getFileIcon = (type: FileType) => {\n  switch (type) {\n    case \"Image\": return <ImageIcon className=\"w-4 h-4 
text-blue-500\" />;\n    case \"Video\": return <Video className=\"w-4 h-4 text-purple-500\" />;\n    case \"PDF\": 
return <FileText className=\"w-4 h-4 text-red-500\" />;\n    case \"Audio\": return <FileAudio className=\"w-4 h-4 
text-amber-500\" />;\n    case \"Document\": return <FileText className=\"w-4 h-4 text-gray-500\" />;\n  
}\n};\n\nexport default function MediaQueue() {\n  // --- State ---\n  const [queue, setQueue] = 
useState<MediaQueueItem[]>([]);\n  const [search, setSearch] = useState(\"\");\n  const [filterStatus, 
setFilterStatus] = useState<QueueStatus | \"All\">(\"All\");\n  const [filterType, setFilterType] = useState<FileType 
| \"All\">(\"All\");\n  const [selectedItem, setSelectedItem] = useState<MediaQueueItem | null>(null);\n  \n  // Init 
data\n  useEffect(() => {\n    const data = Array.from({ length: 15 }, (_, i) => generateMockItem(i));\n    // Sort so 
pending/queued are first\n    data.sort((a, b) => {\n      if (a.status === \"Pending\" || a.status === \"Queued\") 
return -1;\n      return 1;\n    });\n    setQueue(data);\n  }, []);\n\n  // --- Simulated Backend Worker ---\n  
useEffect(() => {\n    const interval = setInterval(() => {\n      setQueue(prev => {\n        let changed = false;\n  
      const newQueue = prev.map(item => {\n          if (item.status === \"Pending\" || item.status === \"Queued\") 
{\n            changed = true;\n            toast.info(`Processing started for ${item.fileName}`);\n            return 
{\n              ...item,\n              status: \"Processing\" as QueueStatus,\n              logs: [{ id: 
Date.now().toString(), timestamp: Date.now(), message: \"Processing Started\" }, ...item.logs]\n            };\n       
   } else if (item.status === \"Processing\") {\n            changed = true;\n            return {\n              
...item,\n              status: \"OCR Running\" as QueueStatus,\n              processingTime: item.processingTime + 
2,\n              logs: [{ id: Date.now().toString(), timestamp: Date.now(), message: \"OCR Running\" }, 
...item.logs]\n            };\n          } else if (item.status === \"OCR Running\") {\n            changed = true;\n  
          return {\n              ...item,\n              status: \"AI Analysis\" as QueueStatus,\n              
ocrStatus: \"Success\",\n              processingTime: item.processingTime + 2,\n              logs: [{ id: 
Date.now().toString(), timestamp: Date.now(), message: \"AI Analysis Started\" }, ...item.logs]\n            };\n      
    } else if (item.status === \"AI Analysis\") {\n            changed = true;\n            const success = 
Math.random() > 0.1; // 90% success rate\n            if (success) {\n              toast.success(`Queue Completed 
Successfully: ${item.fileName}`);\n              return {\n                ...item,\n                status: 
\"Completed\" as QueueStatus,\n                confidenceScore: parseFloat((Math.random() * 10 + 90).toFixed(1)),\n    
            aiSummary: \"Automated analysis completed with high confidence.\",\n                processingTime: 
item.processingTime + 1,\n                logs: [{ id: Date.now().toString(), timestamp: Date.now(), message: \"Result 
Stored & Queue Closed\" }, ...item.logs]\n              };\n            } else {\n              
toast.error(`Processing Failed: ${item.fileName}`);\n              return {\n                ...item,\n                
status: \"Failed\" as QueueStatus,\n                logs: [{ id: Date.now().toString(), timestamp: Date.now(), 
message: \"Processing Failed\" }, ...item.logs]\n              };\n            }\n          }\n          return 
item;\n        });\n        \n        // Update selected item if it's currently open\n        if (changed && 
selectedItem) {\n          const updatedSelected = newQueue.find(i => i.id === selectedItem.id);\n          if 
(updatedSelected) setSelectedItem(updatedSelected);\n        }\n        \n        return changed ? newQueue : prev;\n  
    });\n    }, 4000); // Check/tick every 4 seconds\n\n    return () => clearInterval(interval);\n  }, 
[selectedItem]);\n\n  // --- Actions ---\n  const handleRetry = (id: string, e: React.MouseEvent) => {\n    
e.stopPropagation();\n    setQueue(prev => prev.map(item => {\n      if (item.id === id) {\n        if 
(item.retryCount >= 3) {\n          toast.error(\"Maximum retry limit reached.\");\n          return item;\n        
}\n        toast.info(`Retrying ${item.fileName}...`);\n        return {\n          ...item,\n          status: 
\"Queued\",\n          retryCount: item.retryCount + 1,\n          logs: [{ id: Date.now().toString(), timestamp: 
Date.now(), message: \"Added back to Queue (Retry)\" }, ...item.logs]\n        };\n      }\n      return item;\n    
}));\n  };\n\n  const handleCancel = (id: string, e: React.MouseEvent) => {\n    e.stopPropagation();\n    
setQueue(prev => prev.map(item => {\n      if (item.id === id) {\n        toast.info(`Cancelled ${item.fileName}`);\n  
      return {\n          ...item,\n          status: \"Cancelled\",\n          logs: [{ id: Date.now().toString(), 
timestamp: Date.now(), message: \"Queue Cancelled by User\" }, ...item.logs]\n        };\n      }\n      return 
item;\n    }));\n  };\n\n  const handleDelete = (id: string, e: React.MouseEvent) => {\n    e.stopPropagation();\n    
if (confirm(\"Are you sure you want to delete this media queue item?\")) {\n      setQueue(prev => prev.filter(item => 
item.id !== id));\n      if (selectedItem?.id === id) setSelectedItem(null);\n      toast.success(\"Item deleted from 
queue\");\n    }\n  };\n\n  // --- Stats ---\n  const stats = useMemo(() => {\n    const total = queue.length;\n    
const processing = queue.filter(q => [\"Processing\", \"OCR Running\", \"AI Analysis\"].includes(q.status)).length;\n  
  const completed = queue.filter(q => q.status === \"Completed\").length;\n    const failed = queue.filter(q => 
q.status === \"Failed\").length;\n    const pending = queue.filter(q => q.status === \"Pending\" || q.status === 
\"Queued\").length;\n    \n    const completedItems = queue.filter(q => q.status === \"Completed\" && q.processingTime 
> 0);\n    const avgTime = completedItems.length > 0 \n      ? (completedItems.reduce((acc, curr) => acc + 
curr.processingTime, 0) / completedItems.length).toFixed(1)\n      : \"0.0\";\n\n    return { total, processing, 
completed, failed, pending, avgTime };\n  }, [queue]);\n\n  // --- Filtered Data ---\n  const filteredQueue = 
useMemo(() => {\n    return queue.filter(item => {\n      const matchSearch = 
item.fileName.toLowerCase().includes(search.toLowerCase()) || \n                          
item.id.toLowerCase().includes(search.toLowerCase()) ||\n                          
item.complaintId.toLowerCase().includes(search.toLowerCase());\n      const matchStatus = filterStatus === \"All\" || 
item.status === filterStatus;\n      const matchType = filterType === \"All\" || item.fileType === filterType;\n      
\n      return matchSearch && matchStatus && matchType;\n    });\n  }, [queue, search, filterStatus, filterType]);\n\n 
 return (\n    <DashboardLayout \n      title=\"Media Queue\" \n      subtitle=\"Automated ingestion, processing, and 
AI extraction pipeline.\"\n    >\n      <div className=\"flex flex-col h-[calc(100vh-120px)] overflow-hidden 
bg-gray-50/50 p-2\">\n        \n        {/* --- Stats Dashboard --- */}\n        <div className=\"grid grid-cols-2 
md:grid-cols-3 lg:grid-cols-6 gap-4 mb-6 shrink-0\">\n          {[\n            { label: \"Total Files\", value: 
stats.total, color: \"text-blue-600\", bg: \"bg-blue-50\", icon: <LayoutDashboard className=\"w-5 h-5\"/> },\n         
   { label: \"Processing\", value: stats.processing, color: \"text-amber-500\", bg: \"bg-amber-50\", icon: <RefreshCw 
className=\"w-5 h-5 animate-spin-slow\"/> },\n            { label: \"Pending\", value: stats.pending, color: 
\"text-purple-500\", bg: \"bg-purple-50\", icon: <Clock className=\"w-5 h-5\"/> },\n            { label: 
\"Completed\", value: stats.completed, color: \"text-green-500\", bg: \"bg-green-50\", icon: <CheckCircle2 
className=\"w-5 h-5\"/> },\n            { label: \"Failed\", value: stats.failed, color: \"text-red-500\", bg: 
\"bg-red-50\", icon: <AlertCircle className=\"w-5 h-5\"/> },\n            { label: \"Avg Time (s)\", value: 
stats.avgTime, color: \"text-gray-600\", bg: \"bg-gray-100\", icon: <Activity className=\"w-5 h-5\"/> },\n          
].map((stat, i) => (\n            <div key={i} className=\"bg-white/80 backdrop-blur-md border border-gray-200/60 
rounded-2xl p-4 shadow-[0_4px_20px_-4px_rgba(0,0,0,0.05)] flex flex-col justify-between hover:shadow-lg 
transition-all\">\n              <div className=\"flex justify-between items-start mb-2\">\n                <span 
className=\"text-gray-500 text-xs font-bold uppercase tracking-wider\">{stat.label}</span>\n                <div 
className={`${stat.bg} ${stat.color} p-1.5 rounded-lg`}>{stat.icon}</div>\n              </div>\n              <span 
className={`text-2xl font-black ${stat.color}`}>{stat.value}</span>\n            </div>\n          ))}\n        
</div>\n\n        {/* --- Search & Filters --- */}\n        <div className=\"flex flex-col md:flex-row gap-4 mb-4 
shrink-0 bg-white/80 backdrop-blur-md p-4 rounded-2xl border border-gray-200/60 shadow-sm\">\n          <div 
className=\"relative flex-1\">\n            <Search className=\"w-5 h-5 absolute left-3 top-1/2 -translate-y-1/2 
text-gray-400\" />\n            <input \n              type=\"text\" \n              placeholder=\"Search Queue ID, 
Complaint ID, or File Name...\" \n              value={search}\n              onChange={(e) => 
setSearch(e.target.value)}\n              className=\"w-full pl-10 pr-4 py-2.5 bg-gray-50 border border-gray-200 
rounded-xl focus:outline-none focus:ring-2 focus:ring-blue-500/20 focus:border-blue-500 transition-all text-sm 
font-medium\"\n            />\n          </div>\n          \n          <div className=\"flex gap-3\">\n            
<div className=\"relative\">\n              <Filter className=\"w-4 h-4 absolute left-3 top-1/2 -translate-y-1/2 
text-gray-400\" />\n              <select \n                value={filterStatus}\n                onChange={(e) => 
setFilterStatus(e.target.value as any)}\n                className=\"pl-9 pr-8 py-2.5 bg-gray-50 border 
border-gray-200 rounded-xl focus:outline-none focus:border-blue-500 appearance-none text-sm font-bold text-gray-700 
cursor-pointer\"\n              >\n                <option value=\"All\">All Statuses</option>\n                
<option value=\"Pending\">Pending</option>\n                <option value=\"Processing\">Processing</option>\n         
       <option value=\"Completed\">Completed</option>\n                <option value=\"Failed\">Failed</option>\n      
        </select>\n            </div>\n            <div className=\"relative\">\n              <Filter className=\"w-4 
h-4 absolute left-3 top-1/2 -translate-y-1/2 text-gray-400\" />\n              <select \n                
value={filterType}\n                onChange={(e) => setFilterType(e.target.value as any)}\n                
className=\"pl-9 pr-8 py-2.5 bg-gray-50 border border-gray-200 rounded-xl focus:outline-none focus:border-blue-500 
appearance-none text-sm font-bold text-gray-700 cursor-pointer\"\n              >\n                <option 
value=\"All\">All File Types</option>\n                <option value=\"Image\">Image</option>\n                <option 
value=\"Video\">Video</option>\n                <option value=\"PDF\">PDF</option>\n                <option 
value=\"Audio\">Audio</option>\n              </select>\n            </div>\n          </div>\n        </div>\n\n      
  {/* --- Main Content Area (Table & Side Panel) --- */}\n        <div className=\"flex flex-1 gap-6 overflow-hidden 
relative\">\n          \n          {/* Table Container */}\n          <div className={`flex-1 bg-white/90 
backdrop-blur-md rounded-2xl border border-gray-200/60 shadow-md flex flex-col overflow-hidden transition-all 
duration-300 ${selectedItem ? 'w-2/3 hidden md:flex' : 'w-full'}`}>\n            <div className=\"overflow-auto flex-1 
custom-scrollbar\">\n              <table className=\"w-full text-left border-collapse\">\n                <thead 
className=\"sticky top-0 bg-white/95 backdrop-blur z-10 border-b border-gray-200 shadow-sm\">\n                  
<tr>\n                    <th className=\"py-4 px-4 text-xs font-black text-gray-400 uppercase tracking-widest\">Queue 
ID / Complaint</th>\n                    <th className=\"py-4 px-4 text-xs font-black text-gray-400 uppercase 
tracking-widest\">File Info</th>\n                    <th className=\"py-4 px-4 text-xs font-black text-gray-400 
uppercase tracking-widest\">Status</th>\n                    <th className=\"py-4 px-4 text-xs font-black 
text-gray-400 uppercase tracking-widest\">Priority</th>\n                    <th className=\"py-4 px-4 text-xs 
font-black text-gray-400 uppercase tracking-widest\">AI Service</th>\n                    <th className=\"py-4 px-4 
text-xs font-black text-gray-400 uppercase tracking-widest text-right\">Actions</th>\n                  </tr>\n        
        </thead>\n                <tbody className=\"divide-y divide-gray-100\">\n                  
<AnimatePresence>\n                    {filteredQueue.map((item) => {\n                      const isProcessing = 
[\"Processing\", \"OCR Running\", \"AI Analysis\"].includes(item.status);\n                      \n                    
  return (\n                        <motion.tr \n                          key={item.id}\n                          
layout\n                          initial={{ opacity: 0, y: 10 }}\n                          animate={{ opacity: 1, y: 
0 }}\n                          exit={{ opacity: 0, scale: 0.95 }}\n                          onClick={() => 
setSelectedItem(item)}\n                          className={`hover:bg-blue-50/50 transition-colors cursor-pointer 
group ${selectedItem?.id === item.id ? 'bg-blue-50' : ''}`}\n                        >\n                          <td 
className=\"py-4 px-4\">\n                            <div className=\"font-bold text-gray-900\">{item.id}</div>\n     
                       <div className=\"text-xs text-gray-500 font-medium\">{item.complaintId}</div>\n                 
         </td>\n                          <td className=\"py-4 px-4\">\n                            <div 
className=\"flex items-center gap-2\">\n                              {getFileIcon(item.fileType)}\n                   
           <div>\n                                <div className=\"text-sm font-semibold text-gray-800 max-w-[150px] 
truncate\" title={item.fileName}>\n                                  {item.fileName}\n                                
</div>\n                                <div className=\"text-xs text-gray-500\">{item.fileSize} MB</div>\n            
                  </div>\n                            </div>\n                          </td>\n                        
  <td className=\"py-4 px-4\">\n                            <div className=\"flex flex-col gap-1.5 w-32\">\n           
                   <span className={`inline-flex items-center justify-center px-2.5 py-1 text-xs font-bold border 
rounded-lg whitespace-nowrap ${getStatusColor(item.status)}`}>\n                                {isProcessing && 
<RefreshCw className=\"w-3 h-3 mr-1.5 animate-spin\" />}\n                                {item.status}\n              
                </span>\n                              {isProcessing && (\n                                <div 
className=\"w-full h-1.5 bg-gray-200 rounded-full overflow-hidden\">\n                                  <motion.div \n 
                                   className=\"h-full bg-blue-500\"\n                                    initial={{ 
width: \"0%\" }}\n                                    animate={{ width: \"100%\" }}\n                                  
  transition={{ duration: 4, repeat: Infinity }}\n                                  />\n                               
 </div>\n                              )}\n                            </div>\n                          </td>\n       
                   <td className=\"py-4 px-4\">\n                            <span className={`inline-block px-2 py-1 
text-xs font-bold rounded ${getPriorityColor(item.priority)}`}>\n                              {item.priority}\n       
                     </span>\n                          </td>\n                          <td className=\"py-4 
px-4\">\n                            <div className=\"text-sm font-semibold text-gray-700 flex items-center 
gap-1.5\">\n                              <BrainCircuit className=\"w-4 h-4 text-purple-500\" />\n                     
         {item.aiService}\n                            </div>\n                          </td>\n                       
   <td className=\"py-4 px-4 text-right\">\n                            <div className=\"flex items-center justify-end 
gap-1 opacity-0 group-hover:opacity-100 transition-opacity\">\n                              {item.status === 
\"Failed\" && (\n                                <button onClick={(e) => handleRetry(item.id, e)} className=\"p-1.5 
text-gray-400 hover:text-amber-600 hover:bg-amber-50 rounded-lg transition-colors\" title=\"Retry\">\n                 
                 <RefreshCw className=\"w-4 h-4\" />\n                                </button>\n                      
        )}\n                              {(item.status === \"Pending\" || item.status === \"Queued\") && (\n          
                      <button onClick={(e) => handleCancel(item.id, e)} className=\"p-1.5 text-gray-400 
hover:text-gray-700 hover:bg-gray-100 rounded-lg transition-colors\" title=\"Cancel\">\n                               
   <XCircle className=\"w-4 h-4\" />\n                                </button>\n                              )}\n    
                          <button onClick={(e) => handleDelete(item.id, e)} className=\"p-1.5 text-gray-400 
hover:text-red-600 hover:bg-red-50 rounded-lg transition-colors\" title=\"Delete\">\n                                
<Trash2 className=\"w-4 h-4\" />\n                              </button>\n                            </div>\n        
                  </td>\n                        </motion.tr>\n                      );\n                    })}\n     
             </AnimatePresence>\n                </tbody>\n              </table>\n              {filteredQueue.length 
=== 0 && (\n                <div className=\"flex flex-col items-center justify-center py-20 text-gray-400\">\n        
          <Search className=\"w-12 h-12 mb-4 opacity-20\" />\n                  <p className=\"text-lg 
font-medium\">No items found in the queue.</p>\n                </div>\n              )}\n            </div>\n         
 </div>\n\n          {/* Side Panel (Queue Details) */}\n          <AnimatePresence>\n            {selectedItem && (\n 
             <motion.div \n                initial={{ opacity: 0, x: 50 }}\n                animate={{ opacity: 1, x: 
0 }}\n                exit={{ opacity: 0, x: 50, transition: { duration: 0.2 } }}\n                className=\"w-full 
md:w-1/3 min-w-[350px] bg-white rounded-2xl border border-gray-200/60 shadow-xl overflow-hidden flex flex-col h-full 
absolute md:relative right-0 z-20\"\n              >\n                <div className=\"p-4 border-b border-gray-100 
flex justify-between items-center bg-gray-50\">\n                  <h3 className=\"font-black text-gray-900 flex 
items-center gap-2\">\n                    <Eye className=\"w-5 h-5 text-blue-500\" /> Queue Details\n                 
 </h3>\n                  <button onClick={() => setSelectedItem(null)} className=\"p-1.5 bg-gray-200/50 
hover:bg-gray-200 text-gray-600 rounded-full transition-colors\">\n                    <X className=\"w-4 h-4\" />\n   
               </button>\n                </div>\n                \n                <div className=\"overflow-y-auto 
p-5 space-y-6 custom-scrollbar pb-10\">\n                  {/* Status Banner */}\n                  <div 
className={`p-4 rounded-xl border flex items-center gap-3 ${getStatusColor(selectedItem.status)}`}>\n                  
  <div className=\"flex-1\">\n                      <div className=\"text-xs uppercase font-black tracking-widest 
opacity-70 mb-1\">Current Status</div>\n                      <div className=\"font-bold 
text-lg\">{selectedItem.status}</div>\n                    </div>\n                    {[\"Processing\", \"OCR 
Running\", \"AI Analysis\"].includes(selectedItem.status) && (\n                       <RefreshCw className=\"w-6 h-6 
animate-spin opacity-70\" />\n                    )}\n                    {selectedItem.status === \"Completed\" && 
<CheckCircle2 className=\"w-6 h-6 opacity-70\" />}\n                    {selectedItem.status === \"Failed\" && 
<AlertCircle className=\"w-6 h-6 opacity-70\" />}\n                  </div>\n\n                  {/* Queue Information 
*/}\n                  <div>\n                    <h4 className=\"text-xs font-black uppercase tracking-widest 
text-gray-400 mb-3 border-b pb-1\">Queue Information</h4>\n                    <div className=\"grid grid-cols-2 gap-4 
text-sm\">\n                      <div>\n                        <div className=\"text-gray-500 mb-0.5\">Queue 
ID</div>\n                        <div className=\"font-bold\">{selectedItem.id}</div>\n                      </div>\n 
                     <div>\n                        <div className=\"text-gray-500 mb-0.5\">Complaint ID</div>\n       
                 <div className=\"font-bold text-blue-600 cursor-pointer 
hover:underline\">{selectedItem.complaintId}</div>\n                      </div>\n                      <div>\n        
                <div className=\"text-gray-500 mb-0.5\">Uploaded By</div>\n                        <div 
className=\"font-medium\">{selectedItem.uploadedBy}</div>\n                      </div>\n                      <div>\n 
                       <div className=\"text-gray-500 mb-0.5\">Upload Time</div>\n                        <div 
className=\"font-medium\">{new Date(selectedItem.uploadDate).toLocaleTimeString([], { hour: '2-digit', minute: 
'2-digit' })}</div>\n                      </div>\n                    </div>\n                  </div>\n\n            
      {/* Media Information */}\n                  <div>\n                    <h4 className=\"text-xs font-black 
uppercase tracking-widest text-gray-400 mb-3 border-b pb-1\">Media Information</h4>\n                    <div 
className=\"bg-gray-50 rounded-xl p-4 flex gap-4\">\n                      <div className=\"w-16 h-16 bg-white border 
border-gray-200 rounded-lg flex items-center justify-center shrink-0\">\n                        
{getFileIcon(selectedItem.fileType)}\n                      </div>\n                      <div className=\"flex 
flex-col justify-center overflow-hidden\">\n                        <div className=\"font-bold text-gray-900 
truncate\" title={selectedItem.fileName}>{selectedItem.fileName}</div>\n                        <div 
className=\"text-sm text-gray-500 flex gap-2 mt-1\">\n                          <span>{selectedItem.fileType}</span>\n 
                         <span>&bull;</span>\n                          <span>{selectedItem.fileSize} MB</span>\n      
                  </div>\n                      </div>\n                    </div>\n                  </div>\n\n       
           {/* AI Processing */}\n                  <div>\n                    <h4 className=\"text-xs font-black 
uppercase tracking-widest text-gray-400 mb-3 border-b pb-1\">AI Diagnostics</h4>\n                    <div 
className=\"space-y-3 text-sm\">\n                      <div className=\"flex justify-between items-center p-2 
bg-gray-50 rounded-lg\">\n                        <span className=\"font-medium text-gray-600\">AI Service</span>\n    
                    <span className=\"font-bold\">{selectedItem.aiService}</span>\n                      </div>\n      
                {selectedItem.confidenceScore && (\n                        <div className=\"flex justify-between 
items-center p-2 bg-gray-50 rounded-lg\">\n                          <span className=\"font-medium 
text-gray-600\">Confidence Score</span>\n                          <span className={`font-black 
${selectedItem.confidenceScore > 85 ? 'text-green-600' : 'text-amber-600'}`}>\n                            
{selectedItem.confidenceScore}%\n                          </span>\n                        </div>\n                   
   )}\n                      {selectedItem.aiSummary && (\n                        <div className=\"p-3 bg-blue-50 
border border-blue-100 rounded-lg\">\n                          <div className=\"text-xs font-bold text-blue-700 
uppercase mb-1\">AI Summary</div>\n                          <div className=\"text-blue-900 font-medium 
leading-snug\">{selectedItem.aiSummary}</div>\n                        </div>\n                      )}\n              
      </div>\n                  </div>\n\n                  {/* Logs */}\n                  <div>\n                    
<h4 className=\"text-xs font-black uppercase tracking-widest text-gray-400 mb-3 border-b pb-1 flex justify-between 
items-center\">\n                      Activity Logs <History className=\"w-4 h-4 text-gray-400\" />\n                 
   </h4>\n                    <div className=\"relative border-l-2 border-gray-200 ml-2 space-y-4 pt-2\">\n            
          {selectedItem.logs.map((log, i) => (\n                        <div key={log.id} className=\"relative pl-4 
flex flex-col\">\n                          <div className=\"absolute w-2 h-2 bg-blue-500 rounded-full -left-[5px] 
top-1.5 shadow-[0_0_0_3px_white]\" />\n                          <span className=\"text-xs font-bold 
text-gray-400\">{new Date(log.timestamp).toLocaleTimeString([], { hour: '2-digit', minute: '2-digit', second: 
'2-digit' })}</span>\n                          <span className=\"text-sm font-medium 
text-gray-800\">{log.message}</span>\n                        </div>\n                      ))}\n                    
</div>\n                  </div>\n                </div>\n\n                {/* Actions Footer */}\n                
<div className=\"p-4 bg-gray-50 border-t border-gray-100 flex gap-2 shrink-0\">\n                  <button 
className=\"flex-1 py-2.5 bg-white border border-gray-200 text-gray-700 font-bold rounded-xl shadow-sm 
hover:bg-gray-50 transition-colors flex justify-center items-center gap-2\">\n                    <Download 
className=\"w-4 h-4\" /> Download\n                  </button>\n                  {selectedItem.status === \"Failed\" 
&& (\n                    <button \n                      onClick={(e) => handleRetry(selectedItem.id, e)}\n

type, fileSize: parseFloat((file.size / 1048576).toFixed(1)) }));\n  };\n\n  // ─── Stats 
────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────\n  const stats = useMemo(() => {\n    const total = 
queue.length;\n    const processing = queue.filter(q => [\"Uploading\", \"Processing\", \"OCR Running\", \"AI 
Analysis\"].includes(q.status)).length;\n    const completed = queue.filter(q => q.status === \"Completed\").length;\n 
   const failed = queue.filter(q => q.status === \"Failed\").length;\n    const pending = queue.filter(q => q.status 
=== \"Pending\" || q.status === \"Queued\").length;\n    const done = queue.filter(q => q.status === \"Completed\" && 
q.processingTime > 0);\n    const avg = done.length ? (done.reduce((a, c) => a + c.processingTime, 0) / 
done.length).toFixed(1) : \"—\";\n    return { total, processing, completed, failed, pending, avg };\n  }, 
[queue]);\n\n  // ─── Filtered ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────\n  const filtered = 
useMemo(() => queue.filter(item => {\n    const q = search.toLowerCase();\n    const matchQ = !q || 
item.id.toLowerCase().includes(q) ||\n      item.complaintId.toLowerCase().includes(q) || 
item.fileName.toLowerCase().includes(q);\n    return matchQ &&\n      (filterStatus === \"All\" || item.status === 
filterStatus) &&\n      (filterType === \"All\" || item.fileType === filterType);\n  }), [queue, search, filterStatus, 
filterType]);\n\n  const isActive = (s: QueueStatus) => [\"Uploading\", \"Processing\", \"OCR Running\", \"AI 
Analysis\"].includes(s);\n\n  // ─── Stage helpers ────────────────────────────────────────────────────────\n  const 
getStageState = (item: MediaQueueItem, stageKey: PipelineStage): \"done\" | \"active\" | \"pending\" | \"failed\" => 
{\n    if (item.status === \"Failed\") {\n      const failedIdx = STAGE_ORDER.indexOf(item.currentStage ?? 
\"upload\");\n      const stageIdx = STAGE_ORDER.indexOf(stageKey);\n      if (stageIdx < failedIdx) return 
\"done\";\n      if (stageIdx === failedIdx) return \"failed\";\n      return \"pending\";\n    }\n    if (item.status 
=== \"Completed\") return \"done\";\n    if (!item.currentStage) return \"pending\";\n    const currentIdx = 
STAGE_ORDER.indexOf(item.currentStage);\n    const stageIdx = STAGE_ORDER.indexOf(stageKey);\n    if (stageIdx < 
currentIdx) return \"done\";\n    if (stageIdx === currentIdx) return \"active\";\n    return \"pending\";\n  };\n\n  
return (\n    <DashboardLayout\n      title=\"Media Queue\"\n      subtitle=\"AI-powered automated ingestion, OCR, and 
evidence analysis pipeline.\"\n      actions={\n        <button\n          onClick={() => setShowAddModal(true)}\n     
     className=\"flex items-center gap-2 bg-[#2563EB] hover:bg-blue-700 text-white px-5 py-2.5 rounded-xl text-sm 
font-black uppercase tracking-widest transition-all shadow-lg shadow-blue-500/30 active:scale-95\"\n        >\n        
  <Plus className=\"w-4 h-4\" /> Add File\n        </button>\n      }\n    >\n      <div className=\"flex flex-col 
h-[calc(100vh-130px)] overflow-hidden\">\n\n        {/* ── Stats 
───────────────────────────────────────────────────────── */}\n        <div className=\"grid grid-cols-3 
md:grid-cols-6 gap-3 mb-5 shrink-0\">\n          {[\n            { label: \"Total Files\", val: stats.total, c: 
\"text-blue-600\", bg: \"from-blue-50 to-white border-blue-100\", icon: <LayoutDashboard className=\"w-4 h-4\" /> },\n 
           { label: \"Pending\", val: stats.pending, c: \"text-indigo-500\", bg: \"from-indigo-50 to-white 
border-indigo-100\", icon: <Clock className=\"w-4 h-4\" /> },\n            { label: \"Processing\", val: 
stats.processing, c: \"text-amber-500\", bg: \"from-amber-50 to-white border-amber-100\", icon: <RefreshCw 
className=\"w-4 h-4 animate-spin\" /> },\n            { label: \"Completed\", val: stats.completed, c: 
\"text-green-600\", bg: \"from-green-50 to-white border-green-100\", icon: <CheckCircle2 className=\"w-4 h-4\" /> },\n 
           { label: \"Failed\", val: stats.failed, c: \"text-red-500\", bg: \"from-red-50 to-white border-red-100\", 
icon: <AlertCircle className=\"w-4 h-4\" /> },\n            { label: \"Avg Time\", val: stats.avg === \"—\" ? \"—\" : 
`${stats.avg}s`, c: \"text-gray-600\", bg: \"from-gray-50 to-white border-gray-100\", icon: <Activity className=\"w-4 
h-4\" /> },\n          ].map((s, i) => (\n            <div key={i} className={`bg-gradient-to-br ${s.bg} border 
rounded-2xl p-3.5 flex flex-col gap-1 shadow-sm`}>\n              <div className=\"flex justify-between 
items-center\">\n                <span className=\"text-[10px] font-black uppercase tracking-widest 
text-gray-400\">{s.label}</span>\n                <div className={s.c}>{s.icon}</div>\n              </div>\n          
    <span className={`text-2xl font-black ${s.c}`}>{s.val}</span>\n            </div>\n          ))}\n        
</div>\n\n        {/* ── Search & Filter ──────────────────────────────────────────────── */}\n        <div 
className=\"flex flex-wrap gap-3 mb-4 shrink-0 bg-white border border-gray-200 p-3.5 rounded-2xl shadow-sm\">\n        
  <div className=\"relative flex-1 min-w-[200px]\">\n            <Search className=\"w-4 h-4 absolute left-3 top-1/2 
-translate-y-1/2 text-gray-400\" />\n            <input value={search} onChange={e => setSearch(e.target.value)}\n     
         placeholder=\"Search ID, Complaint ID, File Name…\"\n              className=\"w-full pl-9 pr-4 py-2.5 
bg-gray-50 border border-gray-200 rounded-xl text-sm font-medium focus:outline-none focus:border-blue-400 
transition-all\" />\n          </div>\n          <select value={filterStatus} onChange={e => 
setFilterStatus(e.target.value as any)}\n            className=\"px-4 py-2.5 bg-gray-50 border border-gray-200 
rounded-xl text-sm font-bold text-gray-700 focus:outline-none focus:border-blue-400 cursor-pointer\">\n            
<option value=\"All\">All Statuses</option>\n            {([\"Pending\",\"Processing\",\"OCR Running\",\"AI 
Analysis\",\"Completed\",\"Failed\",\"Cancelled\"] as QueueStatus[]).map(s =>\n              <option key={s} 
value={s}>{s}</option>)}\n          </select>\n          <select value={filterType} onChange={e => 
setFilterType(e.target.value as any)}\n            className=\"px-4 py-2.5 bg-gray-50 border border-gray-200 
rounded-xl text-sm font-bold text-gray-700 focus:outline-none focus:border-blue-400 cursor-pointer\">\n            
<option value=\"All\">All Types</option>\n            {([\"Image\",\"Video\",\"PDF\",\"Audio\",\"Document\"] as 
FileType[]).map(t => <option key={t} value={t}>{t}</option>)}\n          </select>\n        </div>\n\n        {/* ── 
Main: Table + Side Panel ─────────────────────────────────────── */}\n        <div className=\"flex flex-1 gap-4 
overflow-hidden\">\n\n          {/* Table */}\n          <div className={`flex-1 bg-white rounded-2xl border 
border-gray-200 shadow-sm flex flex-col overflow-hidden min-w-0 ${selectedItem ? \"hidden md:flex\" : \"\"}`}>\n       
     <div className=\"overflow-auto flex-1\">\n              <table className=\"w-full text-left border-collapse\">\n  
              <thead className=\"sticky top-0 bg-white z-10 border-b border-gray-100\">\n                  <tr>\n      
              {[\"Queue / Complaint\", \"File Info\", \"Status & Progress\", \"Priority\", \"AI Service\", 
\"Actions\"].map(h => (\n                      <th key={h} className=\"py-3 px-4 text-[10px] font-black text-gray-400 
uppercase tracking-widest whitespace-nowrap\">{h}</th>\n                    ))}\n                  </tr>\n             
   </thead>\n                <tbody className=\"divide-y divide-gray-50\">\n                  <AnimatePresence>\n      
              {filtered.map(item => (\n                      <motion.tr\n                        key={item.id}\n       
                 layout\n                        initial={{ opacity: 0, y: 8 }}\n                        animate={{ 
opacity: 1, y: 0 }}\n                        exit={{ opacity: 0, x: -10 }}\n                        onClick={() => { 
setSelectedItem(item); setActiveTab(\"info\"); }}\n                        className={`cursor-pointer group 
transition-colors ${selectedItem?.id === item.id ? \"bg-blue-50/60\" : \"hover:bg-gray-50/80\"}`}\n                    
  >\n                        {/* Queue / Complaint */}\n                        <td className=\"py-3 px-4\">\n         
                 <div className=\"font-bold text-sm text-gray-900\">{item.id}</div>\n                          <div 
className=\"text-xs text-blue-600 font-medium\">{item.complaintId}</div>\n                          <div 
className=\"text-[10px] text-gray-400 mt-0.5\">{fmtDate(item.uploadDate)}</div>\n                        </td>\n       
                 {/* File Info */}\n                        <td className=\"py-3 px-4\">\n                          
<div className=\"flex items-center gap-2\">\n                            <div className={`w-8 h-8 rounded-lg flex 
items-center justify-center shrink-0 ${FILE_BG[item.fileType]}`}>\n                              
{FILE_ICON[item.fileType]}\n                            </div>\n                            <div 
className=\"min-w-0\">\n                              <div className=\"text-sm font-semibold text-gray-800 truncate 
max-w-[130px]\" title={item.fileName}>{item.fileName}</div>\n                              <div className=\"text-xs 
text-gray-400\">{fmtSize(item.fileSize)}{item.resolution ? ` · ${item.resolution}` : \"\"}</div>\n                     
       </div>\n                          </div>\n                        </td>\n                        {/* Status + 
progress */}\n                        <td className=\"py-3 px-4\">\n                          <div 
className=\"w-32\">\n                            <span className={`inline-flex items-center gap-1 px-2 py-0.5 text-xs 
font-bold border rounded-lg whitespace-nowrap ${STATUS_COLOR[item.status]}`}>\n                              
{isActive(item.status) && <Loader2 className=\"w-3 h-3 animate-spin\" />}\n                              
{item.status}\n                            </span>\n                            {item.progress > 0 && (\n              
                <div className=\"mt-1.5 w-full h-1.5 bg-gray-100 rounded-full overflow-hidden\">\n                     
           <motion.div\n                                  className={`h-full rounded-full ${item.status === \"Failed\" 
? \"bg-red-400\" : item.status === \"Completed\" ? \"bg-green-400\" : \"bg-blue-500\"}`}\n                             
     initial={{ width: 0 }}\n                                  animate={{ width: `${item.progress}%` }}\n              
                    transition={{ duration: 0.5 }}\n                                />\n                              
</div>\n                            )}\n                            {item.progress > 0 && <div className=\"text-[10px] 
text-gray-400 mt-0.5\">{item.progress}%</div>}\n                          </div>\n                        </td>\n      
                  {/* Priority */}\n                        <td className=\"py-3 px-4\">\n                          
<span className={`text-xs font-bold px-2 py-1 rounded-lg ${PRIORITY_COLOR[item.priority]}`}>\n                         
   {item.priority}\n                          </span>\n                        </td>\n                        {/* AI 
Service */}\n                        <td className=\"py-3 px-4\">\n                          <div className=\"flex 
items-center gap-1.5 text-sm font-medium text-gray-700\">\n                            <BrainCircuit className=\"w-4 
h-4 text-purple-500 shrink-0\" />\n                            {item.aiService}\n                          </div>\n    
                      {item.retryCount > 0 && (\n                            <div className=\"text-xs text-amber-600 
mt-0.5\">↩ {item.retryCount}/3 retries</div>\n                          )}\n                        </td>\n            
            {/* Actions */}\n                        <td className=\"py-3 px-4\">\n                          <div 
className=\"flex items-center gap-1 opacity-0 group-hover:opacity-100 transition-opacity\">\n                          
  <button onClick={e => { e.stopPropagation(); setSelectedItem(item); setActiveTab(\"info\"); }}\n                     
         className=\"p-1.5 text-gray-400 hover:text-blue-600 hover:bg-blue-50 rounded-lg\" title=\"View\">\n           
                   <Eye className=\"w-3.5 h-3.5\" />\n                            </button>\n                          
  {item.status === \"Failed\" && (\n                              <button onClick={e => handleRetry(item.id, e)}\n     
                           className=\"p-1.5 text-gray-400 hover:text-amber-600 hover:bg-amber-50 rounded-lg\" 
title=\"Retry\">\n                                <RefreshCw className=\"w-3.5 h-3.5\" />\n                            
  </button>\n                            )}\n                            {(item.status === \"Pending\" || item.status 
=== \"Queued\") && (\n                              <button onClick={e => handleCancel(item.id, e)}\n                  
              className=\"p-1.5 text-gray-400 hover:text-gray-700 hover:bg-gray-100 rounded-lg\" title=\"Cancel\">\n   
                             <XCircle className=\"w-3.5 h-3.5\" />\n                              </button>\n          
                  )}\n                            <button onClick={e => handleDelete(item.id, e)}\n                    
          className=\"p-1.5 text-gray-400 hover:text-red-600 hover:bg-red-50 rounded-lg\" title=\"Delete\">\n          
                    <Trash2 className=\"w-3.5 h-3.5\" />\n                            </button>\n                      
    </div>\n                        </td>\n                      </motion.tr>\n                    ))}\n               
   </AnimatePresence>\n                </tbody>\n              </table>\n              {filtered.length === 0 && (\n   
             <div className=\"flex flex-col items-center justify-center py-20 text-gray-400\">\n                  
<Search className=\"w-10 h-10 mb-3 opacity-20\" />\n                  <p className=\"font-medium\">No items match your 
search.</p>\n                </div>\n              )}\n            </div>\n          </div>\n\n          {/* ── Side 
Panel ─────────────────────────────────────────────────── */}\n          <AnimatePresence>\n            {selectedItem 
&& (\n              <motion.div\n                initial={{ opacity: 0, x: 40 }}\n                animate={{ opacity: 
1, x: 0 }}\n                exit={{ opacity: 0, x: 40, transition: { duration: 0.15 } }}\n                
className=\"w-full md:w-[380px] lg:w-[420px] bg-white rounded-2xl border border-gray-200 shadow-xl flex flex-col 
overflow-hidden h-full shrink-0\"\n              >\n                {/* Panel Header */}\n                <div 
className=\"p-4 border-b border-gray-100 bg-gradient-to-r from-gray-50 to-white flex justify-between items-center 
shrink-0\">\n                  <div className=\"flex-1 min-w-0\">\n                    <div className=\"font-black 
text-gray-900 text-sm\">{selectedItem.id}</div>\n                    <div className=\"text-xs text-blue-600 
font-medium truncate\">{selectedItem.fileName}</div>\n                  </div>\n                  <div 
className=\"flex items-center gap-2 ml-3\">\n                    <span className={`text-xs font-bold px-2 py-0.5 
rounded-lg border ${STATUS_COLOR[selectedItem.status]}`}>\n                      {selectedItem.status}\n               
     </span>\n                    <button onClick={() => setSelectedItem(null)} className=\"p-1.5 bg-gray-100 
hover:bg-gray-200 text-gray-600 rounded-full\">\n                      <X className=\"w-3.5 h-3.5\" />\n               
     </button>\n                  </div>\n                </div>\n\n                {/* Tabs */}\n                <div 
className=\"flex border-b border-gray-100 shrink-0\">\n                  {([\"info\", \"processing\", \"logs\"] as 
const).map(tab => (\n                    <button key={tab} onClick={() => setActiveTab(tab)}\n                      
className={`flex-1 py-2.5 text-xs font-black uppercase tracking-widest transition-colors ${activeTab === tab ? 
\"text-blue-600 border-b-2 border-blue-600\" : \"text-gray-400 hover:text-gray-600\"}`}>\n                      {tab 
=== \"info\" ? \"File Info\" : tab === \"processing\" ? \"Processing\" : \"Logs\"}\n                    </button>\n    
              ))}\n                </div>\n\n                {/* Tab Content */}\n                <div 
className=\"flex-1 overflow-y-auto p-4 space-y-5\">\n\n                  {/* ── FILE INFO TAB 
─────────────────────────────────────── */}\n                  {activeTab === \"info\" && (\n                    <>\n  
                    {/* File Preview */}\n                      <div className={`w-full h-32 rounded-xl flex flex-col 
items-center justify-center gap-2 ${FILE_BG[selectedItem.fileType]}`}>\n                        <div 
className=\"text-4xl\">\n                          {selectedItem.fileType === \"Image\" ? \"🖼️\"\n                    
        : selectedItem.fileType === \"Video\" ? \"🎬\"\n                            : selectedItem.fileType === 
\"PDF\" ? \"📄\"\n                            : selectedItem.fileType === \"Audio\" ? \"🎵\" : \"📑\"}\n               
         </div>\n                        <div className=\"text-xs font-bold text-gray-500\">{selectedItem.fileType} 
File Preview</div>\n                      </div>\n\n                      {/* File Details */}\n                      
<div>\n                        <h4 className=\"text-[10px] font-black uppercase tracking-widest text-gray-400 mb-3 
border-b pb-1\">Media Information</h4>\n                        <dl className=\"grid grid-cols-2 gap-x-4 gap-y-3 
text-sm\">\n                          {[\n                            [\"File Name\", selectedItem.fileName],\n        
                    [\"File Type\", selectedItem.fileType],\n                            [\"File Size\", 
fmtSize(selectedItem.fileSize)],\n                            [\"Resolution\", selectedItem.resolution ?? \"N/A\"],\n  
                          [\"Uploaded By\", selectedItem.uploadedBy],\n                            [\"Upload Time\", 
fmtDate(selectedItem.uploadDate)],\n                          ].map(([k, v]) => (\n                            <div 
key={k}>\n                              <dt className=\"text-gray-400 text-[10px] font-bold uppercase\">{k}</dt>\n     
                         <dd className=\"font-semibold text-gray-800 truncate\" title={v}>{v}</dd>\n                   
         </div>\n                          ))}\n                        </dl>\n                      </div>\n\n        
              {/* Queue Information */}\n                      <div>\n                        <h4 
className=\"text-[10px] font-black uppercase tracking-widest text-gray-400 mb-3 border-b pb-1\">Queue 
Information</h4>\n                        <dl className=\"grid grid-cols-2 gap-x-4 gap-y-3 text-sm\">\n                
          {[\n                            [\"Queue ID\", selectedItem.id],\n                            [\"Complaint 
ID\", selectedItem.complaintId],\n                            [\"Priority\", selectedItem.priority],\n                 
           [\"AI Service\", selectedItem.aiService],\n                            [\"Retry Count\", 
`${selectedItem.retryCount}/3`],\n                            [\"Processing Time\", selectedItem.processingTime > 0 ? 
fmtTime(selectedItem.processingTime) : \"—\"],\n                          ].map(([k, v]) => (\n                        
    <div key={k}>\n                              <dt className=\"text-gray-400 text-[10px] font-bold 
uppercase\">{k}</dt>\n                              <dd className={`font-semibold truncate ${k === \"Priority\" ? 
PRIORITY_COLOR[v as Priority] + \" inline-block px-1.5 py-0.5 rounded text-xs\" : \"text-gray-800\"}`}>{v}</dd>\n      
                      </div>\n                          ))}\n                        </dl>\n                      
</div>\n\n                      {/* AI Summary (if completed) */}\n                      {selectedItem.status === 
\"Completed\" && selectedItem.aiSummary && (\n                        <div className=\"bg-gradient-to-br from-blue-50 
to-indigo-50 border border-blue-100 rounded-xl p-4\">\n                          <div className=\"flex items-center 
gap-2 mb-2\">\n                            <ShieldCheck className=\"w-4 h-4 text-blue-600\" />\n                       
     <span className=\"text-xs font-black uppercase tracking-widest text-blue-700\">AI Summary</span>\n                
            <span className=\"ml-auto text-xs font-black text-green-600 bg-green-100 px-2 py-0.5 rounded-full\">\n     
                         {selectedItem.confidenceScore}% confidence\n                            </span>\n             
             </div>\n                          <p className=\"text-sm text-blue-900 
leading-relaxed\">{selectedItem.aiSummary}</p>\n                        </div>\n                      )}\n             
       </>\n                  )}\n\n                  {/* ── PROCESSING TAB ────────────────────────────────────── 
*/}\n                  {activeTab === \"processing\" && (\n                    <>\n                      {/* Overall 
Progress */}\n                      <div>\n                        <div className=\"flex justify-between text-xs 
font-bold mb-2\">\n                          <span className=\"text-gray-500 uppercase tracking-widest\">Overall 
Progress</span>\n                          <span className={selectedItem.status === \"Completed\" ? \"text-green-600\" 
: selectedItem.status === \"Failed\" ? \"text-red-500\" : \"text-blue-600\"}>\n                            
{selectedItem.progress}%\n                          </span>\n                        </div>\n                        
<div className=\"w-full h-3 bg-gray-100 rounded-full overflow-hidden\">\n                          <motion.div\n       
                     className={`h-full rounded-full ${selectedItem.status === \"Failed\" ? \"bg-red-400\" : 
selectedItem.status === \"Completed\" ? \"bg-green-400\" : \"bg-blue-500\"}`}\n                            initial={{ 
width: 0 }}\n                            animate={{ width: `${selectedItem.progress}%` }}\n                            
transition={{ duration: 0.6 }}\n                          />\n                        </div>\n                      
</div>\n\n                      {/* Pipeline Steps */}\n                      <div>\n                        <h4 
className=\"text-[10px] font-black uppercase tracking-widest text-gray-400 mb-4 border-b pb-1\">AI Pipeline 
Stages</h4>\n                        <div className=\"space-y-3\">\n                          
{PIPELINE_STEPS.map((step, i) => {\n                            const state = getStageState(selectedItem, step.key);\n 
                           const Icon = step.icon;\n                            return (\n                             
 <div key={step.key} className=\"flex items-start gap-3\">\n                                {/* Stage connector */}\n  
                              <div className=\"flex flex-col items-center shrink-0\">\n                                
  <div className={`w-8 h-8 rounded-full border-2 flex items-center justify-center transition-all\n                     
               ${state === \"done\" ? \"bg-green-500 border-green-500 text-white\"\n                                   
   : state === \"active\" ? \"bg-blue-500 border-blue-500 text-white animate-pulse\"\n                                 
     : state === \"failed\" ? \"bg-red-400 border-red-400 text-white\"\n                                      : 
\"bg-white border-gray-200 text-gray-300\"}`}>\n                                    {state === \"done\" ? 
<CheckCircle2 className=\"w-4 h-4\" />\n                                      : state === \"active\" ? <Loader2 
className=\"w-4 h-4 animate-spin\" />\n                                      : state === \"failed\" ? <AlertCircle 
className=\"w-4 h-4\" />\n                                      : <Icon className=\"w-4 h-4\" />}\n                    
              </div>\n                                  {i < PIPELINE_STEPS.length - 1 && (\n                          
          <div className={`w-0.5 h-5 mt-1 rounded-full transition-colors ${state === \"done\" ? \"bg-green-300\" : 
\"bg-gray-200\"}`} />\n                                  )}\n                                </div>\n                  
              <div className=\"flex-1 pt-1\">\n                                  <div className=\"flex justify-between 
items-center\">\n                                    <span className={`text-sm font-bold ${state === \"done\" ? 
\"text-green-700\" : state === \"active\" ? \"text-blue-700\" : state === \"failed\" ? \"text-red-600\" : 
\"text-gray-400\"}`}>\n                                      {step.label}\n                                    
</span>\n                                    <span className={`text-[10px] font-black uppercase px-2 py-0.5 
rounded-full ${state === \"done\" ? \"bg-green-100 text-green-700\" : state === \"active\" ? \"bg-blue-100 
text-blue-700\" : state === \"failed\" ? \"bg-red-100 text-red-600\" : \"bg-gray-100 text-gray-400\"}`}>\n             
                         {state === \"done\" ? \"✓ Done\" : state === \"active\" ? \"Running…\" : state === \"failed\" 
? \"✗ Failed\" : \"Waiting\"}\n                                    </span>\n                                  </div>\n 
                                 {state === \"active\" && (\n                                    <div 
className=\"mt-1.5 w-full h-1 bg-gray-100 rounded-full overflow-hidden\">\n                                      
<motion.div className=\"h-full bg-blue-400 rounded-full\"\n                                        initial={{ x: 
\"-100%\" }} animate={{ x: \"100%\" }}\n                                        transition={{ repeat: Infinity, 
duration: 1, ease: \"linear\" }} />\n                                    </div>\n                                  
)}\n                                </div>\n                              </div>\n                            );\n     
                     })}\n                        </div>\n                      </div>\n\n                      {/* AI 
Sub-results */}\n                      {(selectedItem.ocrStatus || selectedItem.objectDetectionStatus || 
selectedItem.faceDetectionStatus) && (\n                        <div>\n                          <h4 
className=\"text-[10px] font-black uppercase tracking-widest text-gray-400 mb-3 border-b pb-1\">AI Sub-System 
Results</h4>\n                          {[\n                            { label: \"OCR Engine\", status: 
selectedItem.ocrStatus },\n                            { label: \"Object Detection\", status: 
selectedItem.objectDetectionStatus },\n                            { label: \"Face Detection\", status: 
selectedItem.faceDetectionStatus },\n                          ].map(({ label, status }) => status && (\n              
              <div key={label} className={`flex justify-between items-center p-2.5 rounded-lg mb-2 ${status === 
\"Success\" ? \"bg-green-50\" : \"bg-red-50\"}`}>\n                              <span className=\"text-sm 
font-semibold text-gray-700\">{label}</span>\n                              <span className={`text-xs font-bold px-2 
py-0.5 rounded-full ${status === \"Success\" ? \"bg-green-200 text-green-700\" : \"bg-red-200 text-red-700\"}`}>\n     
                           {status}\n                              </span>\n                            </div>\n       
                   ))}\n                        </div>\n                      )}\n                    </>\n            
      )}\n\n                  {/* ── LOGS TAB ──────────────────────────────────────────── */}\n                  
{activeTab === \"logs\" && (\n                    <div>\n                      <h4 className=\"text-[10px] font-black 
uppercase tracking-widest text-gray-400 mb-4 border-b pb-1 flex justify-between\">\n                        Activity 
Log <span>{selectedItem.logs.length} entries</span>\n                      </h4>\n                      <div 
className=\"relative border-l-2 border-gray-200 ml-3 space-y-4\">\n                        
{selectedItem.logs.map((log, i) => (\n                          <div key={log.id} className=\"relative pl-5\">\n

ull.jsonl:644:{"step_index":708,"source":"MODEL","type":"SEARCH_WEB","status":"DONE","created_at":"2026-07-20T12:03:37Z
","content":"Created At: 2026-07-20T12:03:37Z\nCompleted At: 2026-07-20T12:03:42Z\nThe search for \"ocrText 
extractText DocumentContext\" returned the following summary:\nOptical Character Recognition (OCR) is the foundational 
technology used to convert images of text—such as scanned documents, photos, or PDFs—into machine-readable, editable, 
and searchable digital data. While traditional OCR focuses on identifying individual characters, modern document 
processing now leverages AI to incorporate **document context** for higher accuracy and deeper insights.\n\n### 1. 
OCR: Text Extraction\nOCR acts as a bridge between the physical and digital worlds. The process typically involves:\n* 
  **Preprocessing:** Cleaning the image by deskewing, adjusting contrast, or removing noise to improve recognition.\n* 
  **Text Recognition:** Using pattern matching or feature extraction to identify characters, words, and symbols.\n*   
**Digitization:** Converting the visual data into structured formats (like JSON, XML, or searchable PDFs) that can be 
integrated into business systems.\n\n### 2. The Role of Document Context\n\"Raw\" OCR can read text but often 
struggles to understand it. For example, it might identify \"$2,500\" as a string of characters but fail to realize it 
represents a \"Total Amount Due.\" **Document context** refers to the added layer of intelligence—often provided by 
Artificial Intelligence (AI) and Natural Language Processing (NLP)—that helps systems interpret the *meaning* and 
*relationship* of the extracted data.\n*   **Layout Analysis:** Recognizing structural elements like tables, headers, 
footers, and form fields.\n*   **Semantic Understanding:** Interpreting the intent behind the text (e.g., 
distinguishing between an invoice number and a phone number based on their position and surrounding keywords).\n*   
**Error Correction:** Using context (e.g., dictionary lookups or grammar models) to fix inaccuracies that simple 
character recognition might miss.\n\n### 3. Evolution to Intelligent Document Processing (IDP)\nThe industry is 
shifting from basic OCR to **Intelligent Document Processing (IDP)**, which combines OCR with AI to handle complex, 
unstructured documents.\n*   **Why it matters:** Traditional OCR is often brittle when faced with varying layouts or 
handwriting. IDP solutions are \"context-aware,\" meaning they can adapt to different document formats (e.g., invoices 
from different vendors) without needing rigid, pre-defined templates.\n*   **Key Technologies:** Modern systems use 
deep learning, neural networks, and Large Language Models (LLMs) to perform entity extraction, classification, and 
interpretation, transforming raw pixels into actionable business insights.\n\n### 4. Popular Tools & Solutions\nThe 
choice of tool often depends on whether you require a managed enterprise solution or a self-hosted developer 
library:\n*   **Cloud Enterprise APIs:**\n    *   **Google Document AI:** Optimized for document processing with 
built-in, context-aware extractors.\n    *   **Amazon Textract:** Excels at extracting forms, tables, and structured 
data with high precision.\n    *   **Azure Document Intelligence:** Offers powerful pre-built models for specific 
document types like tax forms and insurance cards.\n*   **Open-Source & Developer Tools:**\n    *   **Tesseract:** A 
widely-used, classic open-source engine for general OCR tasks.\n    *   **Unstructured.io:** Focuses on converting 
diverse file types into LLM-ready chunks for RAG (Retrieval-Augmented Generation) pipelines.\n    *   **LlamaParse:** 
A developer-focused tool designed for semantic extraction from complex PDFs.\n\nBy moving beyond simple text 
extraction to **context-aware document understanding**, businesses can significantly reduce manual data entry, 
minimize errors, and automate complex workflows involving invoices, legal contracts, and medical records."}
